Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on dp structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 624. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 154 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the dp space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 624 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 154. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on dp structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 26. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 575 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 26 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the dp structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 575. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
